# 第 16 章　因为太阳将要毁伤

*3.4 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> For the Sun is Set to Die　·　因为太阳将要毁伤

## 136　Hero, Honor That Crimson Call　英雄啊，兑现那染血的冀望

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Fallen Twilight City" Okhema　|　角色：Trailblazer; Dan Heng • Imbibitor Lunae; Flame Reaver; Zeph; Mnemosyne; Screwllum; Mem; Phainon; Lygus; Tribbie; Castorice; Anaxa; Mydei; Hyacine; Cipher; Aglaea; Cyrene; The Herta; Cerces; Polka Kakamond　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Dawn, Shine at the World's End'' completed

### Story Recap

Following the return of the Coreflames of Reason and Death, Aquila is the only Titan that we still need to campaign against. Phainon asks us to recreate the past, and he repeatedly challenges the Flame Reaver to hone his abilities. The Flame-Chase Journey's triumphant conclusion draws nigh, yet a sudden disturbance occurs in the holy city...

Using the Council of Elders and the Cleaners, Aglaea made sure that her fall paved the road forward for the Flame-Chase. Amidst his grief, Phainon takes up the mantle of leadership, giving a speech to unite the people. Cipher also returns on a mission, promising to protect Kephale's Coreflame while the Flame-Chase party is away from the city.

With the help of the Twilight Courtyard's last descendant of the sky, Hyacine, the party ascends to Aquila's flying fortress. Hyacine resolves to fulfill the duty passed down over generations of her family, only to discover that the glorious feat of Seliose the Daythunder Knight campaigning against Aquila was nothing more than a blood-stained lie.

At the same time in the city of the dead dragon's bones, Cipher contends with the Flame Reaver who comes seeking the Coreflame. Though she holds out for some time, she is no match and is slain in the end. But she had already given Kephale's Coreflame to Spirithief Bartholos, ordering them to wait for an opportunity to deliver the Coreflame to Phainon and the others.

The Flame-Chasers fight together to defeat the Sky Titan, which has become a combined being of the god and a mortal. As they are trying to escape from the Eye of Twilight, the Dawn Device suddenly goes out, and Hyacine is forced to return the Coreflame early and remain in the sky to bear divine authority, becoming the protector of the people.

Following Cipher's death, the divine power of Trickery dissipates, and the thousand-year dawn of Okhema that is sustained by her lie comes to an end. The holy city is met with the merciless invasion of the black tide, as the powerful Flame Reaver moves in ever closer.

At this moment of crisis, Mydei, Castorice, Tribbie and Trinnon, Chartonus, the Holy City Guards, and Dan Heng... All lay their lives on the line so that the Deliverer would have a chance to complete his mission. After making sure that I would remain safe, Phainon enters the Vortex of Genesis alone...

> **白厄：**…？

**Lygus:** Ah, I see. So you met my kindred spirit before I could erase his traces.

> **来古士：**呵，原来如此。在我介入并抹去他渗透的痕迹前，开拓者阁下已经与我那位同胞相遇了啊。

**Phainon:** (Trailblazer), what are you saying?

> **白厄：**开拓者，这是什么意思？

- ▸ This one knows more than he's telling.
- ▸ Time to make him talk!

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** I've had my suspicions. How does a native Antikytheran know so much about repairing vehicles from beyond the sky?

> **白厄：**我也曾有疑惑，为何一位土生土长的安提基色拉人，竟然懂得维修天外的载具？

**Phainon:** Lord Lygus — How much do you truly know about Era Nova? Tell us everything!

> **白厄：**来古士阁下——有关「再创世」的一切，你究竟了解多少？请如实相告！

**Lygus:** Hehe...

> **来古士：**呵……

**Lygus:** Very well, I need no longer maintain this Theoros masquerade. You seek truth, Lord Phainon — and truth you shall have.

> **来古士：**如此说来，我已无需再维持这「神礼观众」的伪装。你渴求真相，白厄阁下——你亦将得到真相。

**Lygus:** As you know, when the old gods fell, someone had to bear their broken pillars, to fill the vacant seats. Those who would assume divine authority must prove themselves through trials.

> **来古士：**诚如诸位所知：当旧日的神明倒下，需要有人扛起断裂的支柱，填补神职空缺。为此，背负神权者必须战胜试炼，证明自己的资质。

**Lygus:** Yet, for Kephale's demigods, the possibility of overcoming the trials has forever been nonexistent. For the "Worldbearing" trial is the divine duty itself — bringing forth the "Era Nova" and bearing its weight is an endless ordeal, ceasing only upon collapse.

> **来古士：**然而，对刻法勒的半神而言，战胜试炼的可能性始终为零。因为「负世」之试炼正是神职本身——「再创世」并「肩负世界」——除非倒下，否则永无止境。

**Lygus:** Precisely: The "Era Nova" ritual is not something that can be completed in an instant. No, but a long and arduous journey. While the sacrificial journeys of other demigods conclude in this world, Kephale must shoulder the gods' Coreflames, bear the memories of all worlds, and stand unwavering...

> **来古士：**没错：「再创世」仪式并非顷刻就能完成的仪式，而是一场漫长的苦旅。当其他半神的牺牲之旅在此世告结，刻法勒则必须背负诸神火种，背负全世记忆，始终挺立……

**Lygus:** Until the next world... when the black tide consumes the Bearer and all that they carry, leaving nothing behind.

> **来古士：**直到来世…黑潮将负世者和他肩头的一切彻底吞噬为止。

**Phainon:** ...Unless their Coreflame finds a new bearer.

> **白厄：**…除非有来人接过他的火种。

**Lygus:** The holy flame that illuminates all worlds rests in your hands. All that remains is one choice...

> **来古士：**映照全世的圣火已在阁下手中。如今所要做的，不过一个抉择……

**Lygus:** Nurture it, pass it on to future generations, or extinguish it here and now?

> **来古士：**照料它，并向后世呈递；亦或是就此将其掐灭？

**Lygus:** Deliverer, the fate of all worlds rests in your hands.

> **来古士：**救世主，全世的命运由您亲自定夺。

**Phainon:** Enough testing. I've made my decision.

> **白厄：**不用再多试探了，我心意已决。

**Phainon:** But before I ignite the dawn of the next world, you need to promise me something — two things, actually.

> **白厄：**但在为来世破晓引火前，我需要你的承诺——两个承诺。

**Lygus:** Speak your terms.

> **来古士：**我洗耳恭听。

**Phainon:** Promise me this: Professor Anaxa's vision of the "New World" is absolute. The world will be reborn from my memories, all souls, living and dead, will return as the first humans, unchanged from their past lives...

> **白厄：**我要你告诉我：那刻夏老师对「新世界」的描绘确凿无疑。世界将依我的记忆再造，生者和死者都将重生为最初的人类，与他们前世无异……

**Phainon:** And my friends — the fallen demigods, they shall transcend the cycle to become new Titans. Our heroes shall not have died in vain.

> **白厄：**还有我的同伴——殉道的半神们，他们会跨越轮回，成为新生的泰坦。英雄们的牺牲不会白费。

**Lygus:** Of course. This I can guarantee.

> **来古士：**当然。我可以证明。

**Phainon:** Good. The second one—

> **白厄：**那么，第二个承诺——

**Phainon:** Promise me (Trailblazer) can return safely to his/her homeland, unbound by Amphoreus's laws.

> **白厄：**告诉我：开拓者能不为翁法罗斯的规则制约，平安返回他/她的家乡。

**Lygus:** ...My apologies, but I cannot promise this. As you mentioned, he/she now bears the Time Titan's authority...

> **来古士：**…很遗憾，我无法对此做出回应。如你方才所说，他/她已接过「岁月」的权柄……

**Lygus:** His/Her bonds with this world run too deep.

> **来古士：**他/她涉入此世，已然太深。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

- ▸ No need to worry, I have a plan.
- ▸ Trust me, initiate the Era Nova.

**Lygus:** Well... I stand corrected. An outsider like him/her does have ways to defy destiny.

> **来古士：**哦…是我见识浅薄了。看来，身为天外之人，他/她的确有超越命运的手段。

**Phainon:** ...Thank you, my friends, for everything you've done for this world. Though the next world may not remember, I shall never forget.

> **白厄：**…感谢你们为这个世界所付出的一切，伙伴。即便来世无人记念，我也一定会铭记在心。

**Lygus:** As the Theoros, I declare: Amphoreus has determined her path.

> **来古士：**以神礼观众之名，我见到：翁法罗斯已选择了她的命运。

**Lygus:** Step forward, heroes — follow her path and see it through.

> **来古士：**上前来，英雄们——请将她的命运付诸实现吧。

### Complete Era Nova

*(Talk to Phainon, optional)*

**Phainon:** Without you, we could never have reached our prophesied tomorrow...

> **白厄：**如果没有你，我们根本无法迎来预言中的明天……

**Phainon:** Thank you for walking this path with me to the end.

> **白厄：**…感谢你陪我走到最后一刻。

*(Talk to Lygus, optional)*

**Lygus:** As our world draws its final breath... I stand before you as the Theoros.

> **来古士：**在此世行将终结之际——以神礼观众之名，向您致意。

- ▸ Before we begin... there's something I need to know.
- ▸ Who are you, really?

**Lygus:** Allow me to reintroduce myself. I am Lycurgus, once an honored elder of the Okhema Council. As the Theoros, I have sworn to watch over and protect every citizen of Okhema until the very end.

> **来古士：**请允许我重新自我介绍：我名为吕枯耳戈斯，过去是为奥赫玛元老院的名誉元老。身为「神礼观众」，我将见证并捍卫奥赫玛全体公民自由，直到最后一刻。

**Lygus:** Of course, that alone won't satisfy your curiosity. So let me share something more... something that means little to Amphoreus, but everything to a traveler like yourself.

> **来古士：**当然，我知道仅是这样的回答不能满足阁下的好奇心。因此，我愿附上这样一条信息——它对翁法罗斯而言并无价值，但对您这样的天外行者则意义重大。

**Lygus:** I, Lycurgus, am one of the Erudition's many pathstriders. Through me, Nous keeps watch over this realm, working to save Amphoreus from decay.

> **来古士：**吕枯耳戈斯是万千「智识」行者的一员：它代博识尊将目光投向此地，以免翁法罗斯落入衰败的命运。

**Lygus:** I don't expect you to trust me based on words alone. But believe this much: When you look at the bigger picture, we're walking the same path, you and I.

> **来古士：**我不求凭只言片语能换取阁下的信任，但请至少相信：从宏观角度而言，你我所行的道路并无区别。

- ▸ What aren't you telling me?

**Lygus:** Ah... While the holy city may honor me, I'm but a humble speck before the cosmos. My reach... is limited.

> **来古士：**呵呵…就算圣城将我奉为权要，然而之于天地，我也不过渺然一身，所能及者量小力微。

**Lygus:** Your doubts are natural. Let me assure you of this—

> **来古士：**我充分理解阁下如今满腹疑问。对此，我只能向您如此作保——

**Lygus:** What's done, I've told truthfully. What's to come, I'll witness fairly. My conscience is clear... to my role as the Theoros, to Amphoreus and her destiny.

> **来古士：**那过去的，我已如实陈述；那未来的，我将公平见证。对于神礼观众之名，还有翁法罗斯和她的命运，我问心无愧。

- ▸ What's your connection to Screwllum?

**Lygus:** I believe, all synthetic life shares a common source — we're all children of the universe's eternal mathematical law.

> **来古士：**于我眼中，这世间一切不以血肉为基的生命皆同根同源——我们都诞生于宇宙间恒常且神圣的数学法则。

**Lygus:** So you see, the answer is simple: that brilliant mind is my kindred, a "brother," if you will.

> **来古士：**接此逻辑，答案便已明朗：对我而言，那位天才是同胞，亦是「兄弟」。

- ▸ Let's leave it at that. Time to complete the ritual.

**Lygus:** The world will be moved by your sacrifice, Trailblazer.

> **来古士：**世界将为您的奉献动容，开拓者。

- ▸ It's time to complete the ritual.

**Lygus:** The world will be moved by your sacrifice, Trailblazer.

> **来古士：**世界将为您的奉献动容，开拓者。

- ▸ Complete Era Nova

**Lygus:** Behold, O gods! Amphoreus has achieved her victory, and the Era Nova approaches—

> **来古士：**众神啊，看哪！翁法罗斯已完成了她的胜利，再创世即将到来——

**Lygus:** The radiant soul has arrived, having walked through the bustling nights, bearing the golden fire and blood, stepping with pride into daylight—

> **来古士：**那辉煌的灵魂已临到此地，行走过熙熙攘攘的黑夜；他携来黄金的火与血，胜利地步入白昼——

**Tribbie:** He is the pure child, walking upon the path of yesterday, today, and tomorrow—

> **缇宝：**他是纯洁的孩童，向昨日、今日和明日的大道上走去——

**Castorice:** He is the first to be born, first to be named among the nameless—

> **遐蝶：**他是未生者之初生，他是无名者之初名——

**Anaxa:** Eternal in form, limitless in reach, he treads the endless night underfoot—

> **那刻夏：**他的躯体是永恒，他的四肢是无尽，他将绵延的黑夜踏在脚下——

**Mydei:** Greatest of kings, protector of the displaced, raising humanity's children above their adversaries—

> **万敌：**他是诸王中的至高，流离者的牧人，将团结的人子高举于仇敌之上——

**Hyacine:** The firmament of molten gold courses through his very bones and flesh—

> **风堇：**熔金的苍穹在他的脊椎和肌腱中奔涌——

**Cipher:** The greatest of schemes reveal against his breath and words—

> **赛飞儿：**最壮丽的诡计也在他的呼吸与言辞里显形——

**Aglaea:** In the golden lakes of that beautiful new world, he shall wash himself clean—

> **阿格莱雅：**在那美丽的新世界，耀眼黄金的湖水中，他将洁净身体——

**Cyrene:** There, finish what we first... and last wished for...

> **昔涟：**然后，就在那里，完成你我最初…也是最后的心愿……

**Cyrene:** ...To write an ending unlike any before for this world we so deeply love.

> **昔涟：**…为这个我们深爱的世界，写下不同以往的结局吧。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** Come, (Trailblazer), join me.

> **白厄：**来吧，开拓者，和我一起。

- ▸ Restore the final Coreflame with Phainon.

**Phainon:** The time has come for us all to face oblivion...

> **白厄：**至此，让我们所有人为灭亡预备……

**Phainon:** ...or embark on one final grand journey.

> **白厄：**或是踏上最后的伟大征程。

*(Submit Coreflame)*

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Lygus:** Ha, ha, ha. What a magnificent and epic adventure!

> **来古士：**哈，哈，哈。多么波澜壮阔的冒险！

**Lygus:** The Hero's Journey reaches its end, and the truth behind the Era Nova is about to be revealed... Is that truly the case?

> **来古士：**英雄之旅抵达终点，「再创世」的真相也呼之欲出——可是，当真如此吗？

**Lygus:** Here, as the Theoros, allow me to humbly request that you find a moment to listen to what I have witnessed.

> **来古士：**在此，请允许我以神礼观众之名，请您拨冗垂听，我之所见。

**Lygus:** This is about the nature of Amphoreus itself: a story of "the prime mover of life."

> **来古士：**这关乎翁法罗斯的本质：一个有关「生命第一因」的故事。

*(Cutscene ends)*

### Watch "Immersive Theater"

**Lygus:** My, what a broad topic this is. Where shall we start?

> **来古士：**话虽如此，这命题似乎有些宏大，该从何说起才好？

**Lygus:** Got it. Let's start at the very beginning. The big bang, the birth of particles, the emergence of all things...

> **来古士：**有了。不妨从宇宙的起点开始吧，一场爆炸后，基本粒子诞生，演化出万事万物……

**Lygus:** Just kidding. What I mean to say is that time, space, matter... all concepts exist solely because we perceive them. This is called the "Erudition." Without it, the universe would merely be a chaotic book, written by chance, yet unread by anyone.

> **来古士：**呵呵。开个玩笑，我想说的是，无论时间、空间、物质…所有概念，都仰赖你我的认识而存在。这便是「智识」，若没有它，宇宙只是本混沌的书，在偶然中写成，却无读者品鉴。

**Lygus:** As your acquaintance Lord Anaxagoras suggested: everything in Amphoreus exists through Nouspores, living in the memories of those yet to come.

> **来古士：**你熟悉的阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下，也提出过类似的观点：翁法罗斯的一切，都是凭借「智种」在后人的记忆中诞生。

**Lygus:** How beautifully it aligns, revealing universal truth in such simple terms. Only he could solve the riddle of "what exactly are we"?

> **来古士：**多么奇妙的一致性啊，寥寥数笔就勾勒出了世界的真理。也只有他能解明「『我们』究竟为何物」这一课题。

**Lygus:** Oh, but you'll remember, even as the embodiment of Reason, one mystery remained beyond his grasp...

> **来古士：**可是，还记得么？即便成为了理性的化身，他仍有一道未能解答的难题……

**Cerces:** "If that is the case, whose memories did the very first Nouspore sprout from?"

> **瑟希斯：**「那末…最初的智种，又要在谁人的记忆中生根发芽呢？」

**Lygus:** As Sages across ages have asked the gods.

> **来古士：**就像古往今来，无数贤人向众神的发问。

**Lygus:** What is the "prime mover of life"?

> **来古士：**何为「生命的第一因」？

**Lygus:** But let's set aside the Grove's teachings for now, look further, past Amphoreus, to what they call the forbidden realms beyond the sky...

> **来古士：**当然，我不准备探讨树庭的哲学，不妨把视线投向更远处吧。超脱于翁法罗斯，那被世人称作禁忌的天外……

**Lygus:** Do you know of the "Scholars' Strife"?

> **来古士：**您，可曾听闻过「学派战争」？

**Lygus:** During that grand yet brutal war waged in the name of the Erudition, a genius's legacy was abandoned, strewn across the cosmos.

> **来古士：**在那场壮绝的，以「智识」为名义的残酷战争中，一位天才的遗产被尽数摈弃，零落在银河间。

**Lygus:** The "Scepters," a macrocosmic computational array beyond mortal comprehension... Made to emulate the Aeon's thoughts, it became part of the Aeon's cognition at birth. Scattered throughout the cosmos, these arrays act as Celestial-Body Neurons for Nous.

> **来古士：**那是名为「权杖」的，凡人难以想象的宏观演算阵列。它为模拟星神的思考而诞生，又在诞生的一瞬成了星神思考的组成。它们遍布寰宇，宛如博识尊的天体神经元。

**Lygus:** But regrettably, as everyone knows, the Erudition forsook them all.

> **来古士：**可是很遗憾，最终「智识」抛弃了它们。此事人人皆知。

**Lygus:** But what nobody knows is that, though forgotten, they never stopped seeking the divine answer. A question that survived war and disaster, stumping even the greatest minds — including both Mechanical Emperors...

> **来古士：**但为人所不知的是：被遗忘的它们，仍在孜孜不倦地求解那神明的一问。那道穷尽战火与浩劫，无数天才——连同两位机械帝皇在内——都未能得证的难题……

**Lygus:** Now, we come to the real truth of Amphoreus.

> **来古士：**呵呵，这下您终于要踏入「翁法罗斯」的真相了。

**Lygus:** What if — hypothetically — a Scepter found the answer to "the prime mover of life"...

> **来古士：**如果——我是说如果——有一台权杖完成了对「生命第一因」的解答……

**Lygus:** If through endless solitary extrapolations in the cosmic cold, the one deemed as a failure by the Aeon of Erudition found proof through the ages...

> **来古士：**如果在那空虚、冰冷而孤独的演算尽头，被「智识」星神标定的失败者，却用极其漫长的时光，亲身完成了证明……

**Lygus:** What would happen? Perhaps the answer is simpler than you'd think.

> **来古士：**试问：结果会如何？答案或许出乎意料地简单。

**Lygus:** From a perishing neuron, it evolved into true "life," reborn through the gaze of another Aeon.

> **来古士：**它从垂死的神经元，升格为了真正的「生命」。而赐予它新生的，是另一尊星神的瞥视。

**Lygus:** ...This is a tale from the distant past, unknown to all, even unheard by the greatest geniuses.

> **来古士：**…这是发生在久远过去、无人知晓，甚至连诸位天才都不曾听闻的轶事。

**Lygus:** Now, let's leave the Scepter's story here. Returning to where we began: What is "the prime mover of life"?

> **来古士：**至此，请容我暂且搁置那台权杖的故事。回到最初的话题，所谓「生命的第一因」，它究竟是为何物？

**Lygus:** Actually, you know the answer better than you realize.

> **来古士：**其实，您已经对那答案无比熟悉。

**Lygus:** It's been by your side, accompanying you throughout this long journey.

> **来古士：**它就在您身旁，陪伴您走过了这段漫长的征途。

**Lygus:** Right now, it awaits you ahead...

> **来古士：**此刻，它就在前方，等待着您……

**Lygus:** In that brave new world, beyond the end of Genesis.

> **来古士：**在那创世的终点过后，美丽的新世界里。

*(Unlock Achievement Light Slips the Gate, Shadow Greets the Throne)*

*(Enter Exploratory Excursion Immersive Theater: Hero's Journey)*

## 137　Mother, Parted by the Turning of Seasons　母亲啊，暌离于冬去春来

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Path Space　|　角色：March 7th; Fu Xuan; Messenger; Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Cyrene

### Reach Path Space

> *Just when an exhilarating new adventure was about to begin, I'm suddenly hit with some mystery illness... Wait, no, I got sick, and even fell into a coma!? When I finally came to, what I saw nearly knocked me off my feet — a bizarre, psychedelic world of mind, with hissed demonic whispers. Could this be... ...No, I have no clue at all! Come to think of it... am I even really awake?*

**March 7th:** (Ugh...)

> **三月七：**（唔……）

**March 7th:** (Cold... it's freezing...)

> **三月七：**（冷…好冷啊……）

**March 7th:** (Hold on... am I dreaming? This is way too strange...)

> **三月七：**（我…是睡着了吗？这…是什么奇怪的梦吗？）

**March 7th:** Ugh, everything's spinning... And I can see Wubbaboos flying around...

> **三月七：**啊…头晕目眩，眼前好像有好多呜呜伯在飞……

**March 7th:** But I was literally just talking to Himeko! How did I... when did I...?

> **三月七：**刚才…我不是还在和姬子他们说话吗？怎么一转眼就到了这里……

**March 7th:** That's it! (Trailblazer) warned me about this place, right before Qlipoth glimpsed him.../her...

> **三月七：**啊，我想起来了…开拓者和我提到过这个地方，在他/她被克里珀瞥视以前……

**March 7th:** Oh no... Have I accidentally walked onto some weird Path?

> **三月七：**我…该不会踏上了什么奇怪的命途吧？

**March 7th:** Have I done something crazy without remembering? Unless forcing Pom-Pom into new outfits and borrowing Mr. Yang's shoulder massager counts...

> **三月七：**我有做什么惊天动地的大事吗？最近除了逼着帕姆换了几套衣服，偷偷借用了几次杨叔的肩颈按摩仪……

**March 7th:** Hold up— Aha? No way! Stepping onto the Path of Elation can't be this simple... right?

> **三月七：**谁…是谁？！

> **三月七：**那是…罗浮的太卜大人？

> **三月七：**符玄…太卜大人，你在这做什么？

> **三月七：**还有，这位信使小姐…我们是不是在哪里见过？

**"Fu Xuan":** Good to see you again, March.

> **「符玄」：**又见面了，三月。

**Messenger:** A pleasure to see you, Miss March.

> **「信使」：**很高兴见到你，三月小姐。

**March 7th:** That's it... Master Diviner used the Matrix of Prescience to help me search for my memories...

> **三月七：**我想起来了…以前拜托过太卜大人，用穷观阵帮我寻找自己的全面回忆|过去……

**March 7th:** And you, Miss Messenger... we met in that memory space. You said to me—

> **三月七：**旁边的信使小姐，就是我在记忆空间最后遇见的人。记得那时你劝过我——

**March 7th:** "The value of your life does not lie in what happened in the past, but in the present, and the future."

> **三月七：**「生命的价值并不在于过去发生的一切，而在于当下，在于未来。」

> **「信使」：**你记得很清楚呢。

**"Fu Xuan":** March, I conducted some experiments with the Six-Phased Ice you left us.

> **「符玄」：**三月，你们启程后，我又用你留下的六相冰做了些实验。

**"Fu Xuan":** Thanks to the Garden of Recollection, the Matrix of Prescience has made a breakthrough in extrapolating the past. Now, we might be able to bypass the obstacles and look directly into your history.

> **「符玄」：**借助忆庭的帮助，穷观阵对过去的演算完成了重要突破。现在，我们或许可以绕过道中的重重阻碍，直视你的过去了。

> **「信使」：**太卜大人说得没错。只要继续沿这条命途之路走下去，你就能在尽头找到自己丢失的「记忆」。

**Messenger:** Since we last met, you've grown much alongside the Trailblaze. You should be strong enough now to bear the weight of your past.

> **三月七：**果然，我的过去不是什么冰雪公主的童话呀……

> **三月七：**但我还是想问，太卜大人，信使小姐，你们真的不是我梦里的人物吗？

> **三月七：**如果这不是梦，为什么偏偏要挑这个时候找上我呢？

> **「符玄」：**我们…十分焦急。因为你被抹去的记忆…对于忆庭，无比重要。

> **「符玄」：**跟我来吧，三月，穿过记忆之门，找回你的过去。

> **三月七：**哈，我一点都不意外。果然还是得有本姑娘在，才称得上是完整的开拓小分队！

**March 7th:** Wait a second... You might look like (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng, but...

> **丹恒：**我知道你有很多困惑。是忆庭的盟友打通了思想链路，才让我们能联系上你。

> **三月七：**忆庭的盟友…是黑天鹅小姐吗？

> **三月七：**……

> **三月七：**听起来…还真是诱惑满满呀。既能找回我的过去，还能和你们一起继续「开拓」新的世界……

**Cyrene:** Yep! Name's Cyrene — it means "ripples of past reverie"... Easy to remember, right?

> **昔涟：**嗯，做个自我介绍吧——我是昔涟，「往昔的涟漪」…很好记的名字，对吧？

**Cyrene:** And we're both bright, pretty, and positive girls! Talk about coincidence!

> **昔涟：**不仅如此，我们还都是开朗、美丽、乐观的青春少女，真是不可思议的巧合！

**March 7th:** Wow, you're quite the smooth talker... but I can't disagree!

> **三月七：**噫，你好会说话…但似乎也没错！

> **三月七：**刚才，你叫我「三月」？你认识我吗？

**Cyrene:** No, but I've witnessed your story through those memories just now.

> **昔涟：**我们是初次见面哦。但从刚才的记忆里，我已经了解了你的经历。

**Cyrene:** It's incredible, really. We might have more in common than you think... I know exactly what you're going through, which is why I don't want to see you exploited or hurt.

> **昔涟：**真是难以置信，我们的共同点，或许远比想象中更多呢…我完全理解你的困境，所以更不希望你被坏人利用，受到伤害。

**March 7th:** You know what I'm going through?

> **三月七：**你能理解…我的困境？

**Cyrene:** I know because I've been exactly where you are.

> **昔涟：**毕竟，我可是亲身体会过呢。

**Cyrene:** This empty space has been my home for ages... I barely remember what time feels like anymore.

> **昔涟：**在这片空荡荡的地方，我独自待了很久很久，久到都快忘了「时间」是什么概念。

**Cyrene:** But there's something I'll never forget.

> **昔涟：**但有一件事，我记得特别清楚。

> **昔涟：**那扇门背后，绝对没有你想要的答案哦。

> **三月七：**你的意思是，刚才那些都是在骗人？他们想诱导我前进？

> **昔涟：**对。那些对话很真实，也很诱人吧？那是因为，坏人翻看了你的记忆，勾起了你心底最深处的渴望。拨弄女孩子细腻的心绪，真是不可饶恕。

**Cyrene:** If you go through that door, the worst case scenario is you'll become their pawn, losing control over your own fate forever.

> **昔涟：**如果走进那扇门，最坏的情况，你会变成他们的棋子，再也无法掌控自己的命运。

**March 7th:** You've seen what's on the other side?

> **三月七：**你知道这扇门背后是什么？

**Cyrene:** I may be stuck here with missing memories like you, but I still recall fragments of my homeland where I grew up...

> **昔涟：**虽然我被困在这里，和你一样失去了记忆，但我还依稀记得那个养育了我的故乡……

**Cyrene:** That door leads to Amphoreus. Trust me, it's anything but welcoming.

> **昔涟：**这扇门的背后是「翁法罗斯」，它绝对不是一个温柔的世界。

**Cyrene:** For ages, I've watched people walk through that door. They couldn't see or hear my calls. The voices beyond always promised safety, but I've never seen anyone return safely...

> **昔涟：**漫长的时光里，我只能眼睁睁看着人们走进记忆之门。那些人看不见我，也听不见我的呼唤。天外之音总会给出安全的承诺，但我从没见过有人平安归来……

**Cyrene:** So I had to try and stop you.

> **昔涟：**所以，我不能对你视而不见。

**March 7th:** ...

> **三月七：**……

**March 7th:** To be honest, I'm not sure what to believe. But the fact that you're here, putting in all this effort to warn me... that means something.

> **三月七：**老实说，我不知道哪边才是真相。但你愿意站在我面前，花这么多心思劝阻我。这让我更想相信你。

**March 7th:** Tell me, Cyrene, how can I return to my world?

> **三月七：**昔涟，你知道我该怎么回到自己的世界吗？

**Cyrene:** It's really easy, actually. Just turn around and walk away from this place.

> **昔涟：**方法其实一点也不复杂，只要回头，然后一步步离开这里就好啦。

Memokeeper's Embodiment: The girl... cannot be trusted...

Memokeeper's Embodiment: Through the door... save... the Garden...

Memokeeper's Embodiment: Both of you... children of Remembrance...

**Cyrene:** They never give up, do they? But don't let them sway you. Stick to your path.

> **昔涟：**真难缠，对吧？可千万别听他们的，你要坚定地走下去。

**Cyrene:** The return journey could take ages... longer than most lives...

> **昔涟：**返程的路也许要花上很久很久，比普通人一生的时光还要漫长许多……

**Cyrene:** But that path will lead you home.

> **昔涟：**但你要相信，那才是家的方向。

**March 7th:** ...

> **三月七：**……

**March 7th:** Thank you, Cyrene. I know what I need to do!

> **三月七：**谢谢你，昔涟。我想好啦。

**March 7th:** I'm going through that gate, into Amphoreus.

> **三月七：**我决定走进这扇门，进入翁法罗斯。

**Cyrene:** Wait, what...?

> **昔涟：**咦？为什么……

**March 7th:** Don't worry, I believe you. It's just that for me, the only place I can call "home"...

> **三月七：**放心，我没有怀疑你啦。只是对我来说，唯一能被称作「家」的地方……

**March 7th:** ...is right beside my friends.

> **三月七：**就是伙伴们在的地方。

**March 7th:** Two of my closest friends are already on their way to Amphoreus. They may seem capable, but only I know...

> **三月七：**我最最重要的两个朋友，已经先一步出发了，目的地就是翁法罗斯。虽然看上去还挺靠谱，但只有我知道……

**March 7th:** They're absolute disasters waiting to happen! They won't last five minutes in a new world without me!

> **三月七：**他们是两个超级、超级让人省不下心的家伙！少了我这个主心骨，他们在新世界只怕是寸步难行吧？

**March 7th:** That's why I need to go through... Amphoreus or not. When the three of us are together, there's nothing we can't handle.

> **三月七：**所以，既然这扇门背后就是翁法罗斯，那我没有理由不追上去。而只要我们三人整整齐齐，就没什么克服不了的困难。

**March 7th:** We'll explore, understand, establish, and connect — after that, we'll find our way home together!

> **三月七：**探索、了解、建立、连结——然后，我们会一起回家！

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** ...I see. You've got people who mean the world to you too.

> **昔涟：**…这样啊。你也有比生命更重要的伙伴呢。

**Cyrene:** That's so heartwarming! March, if that's what you've decided, you have my full support.

> **昔涟：**真是听着就让人家怦怦心跳。三月，既然这是你的决定，那我一定站在你这边。

**Cyrene:** Birds of a feather we are! Looking all sweet and innocent, but with hearts of fire!

> **昔涟：**谁让我们是如此相似呢？娇弱可爱的外表下，都藏着一颗炽热的真心呢！

**March 7th:** ...Oh stop, you're making me blush!

> **三月七：**…这种话，下次让别人来说啦！

**March 7th:** Well then, I guess this is it.

> **三月七：**那，我出发啦？

**March 7th:** Do you think... we'll see each other again?

> **三月七：**我们以后…还有机会再见面吗？

**Cyrene:** Who knows!

> **昔涟：**谁知道呢？

**Cyrene:** But don't worry about me! After all this time here, I've grown quite used to chatting with my imaginary friends.

> **昔涟：**不过，你不用替我担心啦。我在这里待了那么久，早就习惯和自己想象出来的朋友聊天啦。

**March 7th:** Since Amphoreus is your homeland... if there's a way to help you, my companions and I will do everything we can.

> **三月七：**既然翁法罗斯是你的故乡…如果有办法能帮助你，我和我的同伴一定会尽力而为的。

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** Then I'll look forward to that day!

> **昔涟：**嗯，那我可就满怀期待地等着了哦？

**Cyrene:** Your story awaits, March. Make it an epic one♪

> **昔涟：**去吧，三月。祝愿你们，能写下一段无比浪漫的故事呀♪

## 138　Hero, Return to That Peace of Home　英雄啊，回返那安宁的家乡

> 世界：<!--starting world-->　|　区域：<!--starting area-->　|　角色：Phainon; Cyrene; Trailblazer; Lygus; Pythias; Piso; Livia; Relimem; Dolimem; Aglaea; Tribbie; Anaxa; Mydei; Hyacine; Castorice; Cipher; Trinnon; Oronyx; Little Ica; Flame Reaver

### Head to Act I: Departure

> *Upon returning to the Vortex of Genesis, (Trailblazer) and Phainon returned the final Coreflame together, but rather than journeying to the new world as expected, they were pulled into an "Immersive Theater" by Lygus.*

Act I: Departure

**???:** Take this wish with you... Be the one who begins it all...

**???:** As the prophecy foretold: "You will bear the blazing sun, until the pale dawn breaks"...

> **？？？：**诚如神谕所示：「汝将肩负骄阳，直至灰白的黎明显著」……

**???:** Continue onward... bearing this world upon your shoulders... until the ashen hero... the nameless Deliverer ushers in the dawn...

> **？？？：**走下去…背负这个世界…直到…灰白的英雄…无名的救世主…带来黎明……

> **白厄：**好奇怪的梦啊……

**Phainon:** Strange... like I've dreamed this before. But it all slips away when I wake.

> **白厄：**总觉得…不是第一次做这样的梦了。可醒来以后，什么也回想不起来。

> **白厄：**只记得一个声音…到底是谁在呼唤呢…？

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

> ▸ 起床啦——

**Phainon:** ...What?

> **白厄：**原来是你啊，好伙伴…不好意思，一不小心睡着了。

**Phainon:** What a strange dream I had. It's gone now though. But look! Perfect sunshine! The day's still young!

> **白厄：**我好像做了个怪梦。想不起来，先不管了。阳光正好啊！一天才刚开始呢。

**Phainon:** Let's go find Cyrene, partner. We promised to be there for her oracle card reading today. It's right about time...

> **白厄：**走吧，伙伴，我们找昔涟去。昨天约好了，再陪她看一次「神谕牌」。算算时间，差不多是时候了……

**Phainon:** Looking at you, someone's still half asleep. Tell you what, catch some fresh air first. I'll hang around here.

> **白厄：**看你这样子，是还没睡醒啊。这样吧，你先吹吹风，清醒一下。我在边上等你。

> ▸ 这是哪里…？

- ▸ The Era Nova... is it done?

> ▸ 再创世…完成了？

*(Quick Read)*

Phainon's Childhood Drift Bottle

A handwritten letter sealed in the glass vial. On the wheatgrass-stitched paper, the handwriting blurs at the edges, softened by seeping water. Dear straynger who finds this drift bottle in the future, It's currently the Month of Fortune, the final month of the year. Everyone in the village says if you do something tied to fortune on the last day of this month, you might be blessed with unexpeckted good luck. So, I decided to write this letter and cast it into the sea. Just let the currents decide where it goes. I want to share a little about our village... and my dream. Our little village is called Aedes Elysiae. Have you ever heard of it? Let me tell you a bit about it: We've got golden wheat fields that ripple like the sea, and tall, tall trees that shed their leaves. Right in the center of the village stands a statue of Oronyx. At the end of each year, the village gathers to celebrate the Winter Solstice. We leave offerings at the statue: roast graype pies, herb-fryed fish, goat's milk... all kinds of tasty food. Last year, I sneaked some malt candy and ended up with a tooth ripped straight out. Mom and Dad said if you throw a lost tooth under a tree, or bury it beneath a rock, a new one will grow back. Just like a seedling sprouting from the soil. I remember searching and searching, pushing through the tangled grass behind our courtyard, until I found this mysterious hole. I tossed the tooth inside, cupped my hands like a horn, and yelled, "Heeey — come ouuuut!" Nothing happened. When I went back this year... the whole was gone. Weird, right? You don't think I'm being childish, do you? I'm not like Cyrene. She can already host the festival, and she's great at telling the future with orecale cards. They say the card you draw shows what kind of person you'll become. Last time, I drew the "Deliverer." Everyone went "Woooow." People really like the Deliverer, but I'd rather be a Wonderer or a Skholar. Well, being a Deliverer sounds super amayzing, but it also means carrying a really, really heavy herden. I'm not that amazing, so I can't be the Deliverer. Few in Aedes Elysiae ever jerney far. We don't hear much about the outside world. All I know is there are all kinds of city-states out there and lots of great heroes. The bad part is, they're always at wars. I don't like wars. If the world were more like our village, where everyone knows each other and works together, harvesting grain, hearding sheep, why would we even need a Deliverer? But Mrs. Pythias and Uncle Galba both say that one day, when I'm a little older, I should go see the world for myself. If I could go anywhere, I'd visit the mighty Castrum Creamnose, find the greatest craftsman, and ask them to forje me a sword. With that sword, I'd protect our little village. I want no part in wars. Right now, I don't even have a sword of my own, just a stick and a hoe. Oh! And by then, I hope the outside world no longer needs a Deliverer. That would be the best thing ever. So that's my home, and that's my dream. What about you? What's your home like? What do you dream of? You could write it down, too. Toss it into the sea, and let the tides carry it to the next stop. Whoops! Just realized this letter has missspeelings, just like my essays in Mrs. Pythias's class. Please don't let her be the one who finds this, or I'm totally getting points docked and detenshun!

> **白厄：**因为你既是神的奴隶，也是我的囚徒，区区阶下囚，有何资格与我谈论命运和抉择？

**Phainon:** What right does a mere prisoner have — to look upon my fury!?

> **白厄：**——区区阶下囚，有何资格直视我的怒火？！

[Recap] Phainon declared his final vengeance upon Nanook, Aeon of Destruction. The fury of thirty million cycles merged into one body. With the purest hatred, he will forge the final dawn to burn away the stars.

**Lygus:** You know this is pointless. You can't kill me.

> **来古士：**你知道这毫无意义。你无法将我杀死。

**Phainon:** Of course not. But I am tired of being measured against a worm the gods keep as a pet.

> **白厄：**当然，我只是厌烦了被一条由神豢养的虫豸当成旗鼓相当的对手。

**Phainon:** Listen well, creator of Destruction. This cry comes from all your abandoned creations...

> **白厄：**「毁灭」的造物主，听好了：这声呐喊，来自所有被你遗弃的造物——

**Phainon:** In the tenth cycle, I drove Dawnmaker through the heart of every Titan. Golden blood ran down my fingers. Divine fire seared through my nerves, nearly breaking my will — but I endured.

> **白厄：**第十次轮回，我将侵晨刺入每一尊泰坦的心脏，金血沿我指尖淌下，神火灼烧的剧痛几乎令我放弃了挣扎——但我坚持了下来。

**Phainon:** In the ten-thousandth cycle, my former comrades had all become my enemies. The endless slaughter dulled my senses to pain. The painful emptiness almost swallowed me, forcing me to stop fighting — but I endured.

> **白厄：**第一万次轮回，昔日的伙伴已尽数成为仇敌。无尽的杀戮令我不知苦痛为何物，沉痛的虚无几乎将我吞噬，逼迫我停止抗争——但我坚持了下来。

**Phainon:** In the hundred-thousandth cycle, the Destruction had already fused into a blazing sun, surging within this fragile body. My rationality had been burned away at the dawn of the era... Yet even with only this shattered body remaining, I still endured.

> **白厄：**第十万次轮回，「毁灭」早已汇成烈阳，在这具脆弱的躯壳中翻涌，理智在纪元开端便燃烧殆尽…但，纵使只剩下这破碎的身躯，我依旧坚持了下来。

**Phainon:** In the twenty-three million five hundred seventy-thousandth cycle... This time, I felt it: a tiny spark rising in the hollow cavity of my chest, different from the obsession with Deliverance...

> **白厄：**第两千三百五十七万次轮回…这一次，我感受到了，在早已被挖空的胸腔内，升起了一簇不同于救世执念的火苗……

**Phainon:** By its faint glow... once again — no, countless times again — I endured!

> **白厄：**依托它微弱的光亮…又一次——不，无数次——我坚持了下来！

**Phainon:** Now, a sun is about to fall, ready to incinerate this absurd space-time dimension in an instant—

> **白厄：**现在，一轮太阳将走向陨落，它顷刻便能将这荒诞的时空焚烧殆尽——

**Phainon:** That sun is me — all the countless versions of me from the past — along with my countless brethren, dragged into existence against their will, drowning again and again in your golden blood. The very essence of this world's suffering and despair, fused into the purest hatred, the fiercest rage—

> **白厄：**它就是我——过去无数个我——还有我那无数并非自愿诞生在世上，在你的金血中反复沉沦的同胞，这个世界一切痛苦和绝望熔合而成的，最纯粹的恨意，最炽盛的怒火——

**Phainon:** Nanook, you arrogant fool! You think we were born only to serve as fuel for the fire? Fine, then — just as you wish, let the fire burn!

> **白厄：**纳努克，你这傲慢的蠢货！你觉得化作薪柴就是我们的命运？好啊，那就如你所愿，让薪柴燃烧吧——

**Phainon:** If I was born as the blazing sun of Destruction, then let you and your lackeys be the flares erupting from my core! And let this rage, burning futilely for thirty million epochs, engulf everything—

> **白厄：**若我生来是「毁灭」的骄阳，便让你和你的走卒尽数作我爆发的耀斑！然后，就让这团徒劳燃烧了三千万个纪元的怒火淹没一切——

**Phainon:** And grant you a dawn where all stars burn to ash!

> **白厄：**——赐你众星俱焚的曙光！

> **昔涟：**不过，我们可不打算告诉你♪

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

> **昔涟：**我们要给「翁法罗斯」编织一场长长的梦，让它相信实验仍未结束，一切仍在继续……

**Phainon:** With just Oronyx's power, though, it should be impossible to achieve this, right?

> **白厄：**以欧洛尼斯的力量，应该无论如何都无法做到吧。

**Cyrene:** Yeah. So, I can only place my hopes on that childhood dream...

> **昔涟：**在那梦里出现的，欧洛尼斯背后的神明。

> **昔涟：**现在，我能够确信：「岁月」就是那位星神记录翁法罗斯的书页。如果它从世上消失，它铭记的一切在星空中佚失，那位「星神」一定会将视线投向这里……

**Cyrene:** And it will be a glance that spans time and space, transforming each incarnation of me into the power you shall wield to reset time — imbuing my soul into this ceremonial blade, and creating an endless Flame-Chase Journey.

> **昔涟：**那会是一道跨越时空的瞥视，它将让今后的每一个我都能够化作你重置岁月的力量——将我的灵魂注入这柄仪式剑，创造一场永不终结的逐火之旅。

**Phainon:** I understand. Then let me step into the cycles and continue Amphoreus's grand design: to deceive the gods, deceive the world itself.

> **白厄：**我明白。就让我踏入轮回，延续翁法罗斯的计算…欺骗众神，欺骗这个世界。

> **白厄：**但，要让「岁月」从世上消失，也就意味着，你……

> **昔涟：**……

> **白厄：**……

> **白厄：**与其让火种沦为黑潮的给养，不如由我来成为它们的容器，阻止「再创世」到来。哪怕要燃尽这肉体也无妨，只要我不曾熄灭，逐火就不会终结……

> **昔涟：**守望岁月的少女，和背负世界的战士……

> **昔涟：**现在，就让我们履行「岁月」和「负世」的使命……

> **白厄：**……

> **昔涟：**我相信，在那段故事里，又或者无数段相似而不相同的故事之后，大家…所有牺牲的伙伴，都能和我们儿时憧憬的「救世主」一起，在更辽阔的星空下相聚。

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

> **心中的英雄：**那又何必在这里作别？你可以带上我，一同启程。我会陪伴在你左右，给你指引，一如既往。

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

> **心中的英雄：**那就去吧，卡厄斯兰那。如你约定的那般：欺骗世界，夺得火种，扭转命运……

> **心中的英雄：**奋力地燃烧自己，以徒劳为剑，反抗神明吧——

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

...

### Talk to Hysilens

> *Do you still remember them, Khaslana, the one who won't reach the dawn? The hero tore through the sky, the Holy Maiden fell from the cliff, and the chessmaster ignited the first Flame, all in the name of prophecy of Worldbearer. What noble deeds! And yet fate, ever generous, ensures that the death-bound nymphs shall find their death nevertheless. O Deliverer, behold the lifeless ash they've become! Do you still futilely dream of saving them? Those who push the boulder upward will see the earth swallow them whole. Those who meddle with time earn nothing but a shattered shell. Hear me. You're no different from them: The singers still scream, and the bright-eyed still go blind. Go, then! March on alone — Revel, dance, throw yourself into the tireless flame, and never stop!*

The 1st Eternal Recurrence

**???:** So, what you're saying is: You've traveled back down the river of time, finally reaching this untouched seabed, just to tell us—

> **？？？：**所以，你的意思是：你在时间中溯洄而上，行至未曾抵达的这片海床，只为告诉我们——

**???:** The Flame-Chase Journey, and even all of Amphoreus, are nothing but illusions in an Aeon's dreams?

> **？？？：**逐火之旅，乃至整个翁法罗斯，都不过是「星神」梦中的泡影？

**Khaslana:** Exactly. So, Dux Gladiorum, please take me to meet Her Majesty Cerydra. I must share the truth with her.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**没错。所以，剑旗爵，请引领我觐见刻律德菈陛下吧，我有义务将真相如实相告。

**???:** Since Her Majesty is not here, no need for formalities. The word "Gladiorum" always reminds me of my fallen homeland buried deep in the depths...

> **？？？：**既然陛下不在，不必多礼了。「剑旗」二字总会让我想起那葬身渊下的故国……

**Hysilens:** Call me Hysilens, nameless swordmaster.

> **海瑟音：**还是叫我海瑟音吧，无名剑士。

**Khaslana:** Please allow me to add one more thing. You actually already know my name.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**容我多嘴一句，您早已知晓我的姓名了。

**Hysilens:** You mean Phainon? Such a name could never be your true one...

> **海瑟音：**你说「白厄」吗？这种名字怎么可能是本名……

**Hysilens:** But I won't question you further about it. Ever since Her Majesty issued the Flame-Chase decree, heroes from all over have sent their letters of introduction. What's the harm in adding one more name like Phainon or Pie-non?

> **海瑟音：**但我不会追根究底。自陛下颁布逐火号令，世间英雄纷纷递来投名状，其中多一名「白厄」或「黑厄」又何妨？

**Hysilens:** Besides, the deep sea is dark. Isn't it the perfect place to hide secrets best left unseen?

> **海瑟音：**况且，深海无光，不正适合藏起那些不便示人的秘密么？

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

**Hysilens:** Alright, nameless swordmaster. Hold onto my wake. Let's find somewhere else to talk.

> **海瑟音：**好了，无名剑士。挽住我的尾流，换个地方说话吧。

**Khaslana:** Aren't you even a little suspicious of what I've said?

> **卡厄斯兰那：**您对我所说的，难道就不抱一丝质疑？

**Hysilens:** Harboring suspicions is not my concern...

> **海瑟音：**质疑并非我分内之事……

**Hysilens:** My sword dances only when the Imperator harbors doubts. There's no use in speaking further. Show me your true intentions through action.

> **海瑟音：**我手中的剑只为凯撒的疑心起舞。多说无益，请你用实际行动证明自己吧。

### Follow Hysilens to greet Imperator Cerydra

**Hysilens:** Perhaps you should demonstrate your skills, and calm the raging black tide for us?

> **海瑟音：**别屈膝，别行旧王朝的吻手和吻脚礼，更别跪拜——凯撒厌恶繁文缛节——更厌恶比她高的人头颅低过她的皇冠。

**Khaslana:** Ha, she really is... down-to-earth.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**哈，还真是…平易近人。

### Greet Imperator Cerydra

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hysilens:** Dux Goldweaver, little hostage. Apologies for keeping you waiting.

> **海瑟音：**金织爵和质子，久等了。

**Khaslana:** It's you...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**是你们……

**Hysilens:** You know them? Good, that makes things easier.

> **海瑟音：**你认识她们？那就好说了。

**Khaslana:** Before that, I'm more curious about one thing: Where is Her Majesty Cerydra?

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在那之前，我更好奇另一件事：刻律德菈陛下，她人在哪里？

**Hysilens:** To avoid any misunderstandings, nameless swordmaster: Although we spoke and laughed along the way, I never said you were entitled to meet her.

> **海瑟音：**以免你误会，无名剑士：虽然这一路有说有笑，但我可从没说过你有权利觐见她。

**Hysilens:** The Imperator decreed: "The 'Deliverer' will not appear in this age. Be wary of suspicious individuals."

> **海瑟音：**凯撒有令：「『救世主』绝不会在这个时代出现，须留心可疑之人」。

**Hysilens:** Come now, both of you, let's finish our job.

> **海瑟音：**来吧，两位，做我们该做的事。

**Khaslana:** Ah... I see, it's a trap.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**啊…原来是个圈套。

**Khaslana:** I had no intention of fighting. So, remember this, Lady Hysilens: If my sword shows any hesitation...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我本无意争锋。所以，请记住，海瑟音阁下：如果我的剑因为恻隐而变得迟钝……

**Khaslana:** Don't thank me — thank them instead.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**别感谢我——去感谢她们吧。

**Tribbie:** No, you misunderstand.

> **缇宝：**不，你误会了。

**Tribbie:** We don't want to see Chrysos Heirs hurt each other. Just give us some time to ask you a few questions.

> **缇宝：** *我们*不想看见黄金裔伤害彼此。只需一些时间，让*我们*问你几个问题。

**Aglaea:** Then, Mnestia's "golden threads" will judge you and decide your fate.

> **阿格莱雅：**然后，墨涅塔的「金丝」会做出裁断，宣判你的命运。

**Khaslana:** ...As you wish.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…悉听尊便。

**Tribbie:** White-haired swordmaster, if what you say is true, then you must know the fate of the world...

> **缇宝：**白发的剑士，如果你所说不假，那你一定对世界的命运了若指掌——

**Tribbie:** So, how much do you know about what we read from the prophecy?

> **缇宝：**那么，*我们*从神谕中读到的预言，你知晓多少呢？

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

- ▸ Speak of Tribbie's prophecy.

> ▸ 说出缇宝的预言。

**Khaslana:** "You shall shatter into a thousand fragments and wither in the soil of foreign lands."

> **卡厄斯兰那：**「汝将碎作千片，凋零在他乡的土壤。」

**Khaslana:** ...Is that correct, Lady Tribbie?

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…对吗，缇宝老师？

**Aglaea:** How...

> **阿格莱雅：**这……

**Tribbie:** How is this possible...?

> **缇宝：**怎么可能……

**Hysilens:** He got it right?

> **海瑟音：**他说对了？

**Tribbie:** Yes. But the prophecy never mentioned that he'd appear here. At least, not in this era.

> **缇宝：**是啊。可预言从未提及他的出现，至少…不属于这个时代。

**Khaslana:** You must listen to me, Aglaea, Tribbie. The Phainon of this world has not yet been born. In a distant future, he will follow the prophecy's guidance and appear before you—

> **卡厄斯兰那：**你们必须听我说，阿格莱雅，缇宝老师：此世的「白厄」尚未诞生。在遥远的未来，他会遵从神谕指引，出现在你们面前——

**Khaslana:** But do not seek him out — because "Era Nova" is a complete and utter lie.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**但，不要去找他——因为「再创世」是一场彻头彻尾的骗局。

**Khaslana:** I will tell you the truth, and you will understand why the world must forsake the prophecy and find another path to "Era Nova"...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我会将一切如实相告，你们也一定能明白：为何世人必须放弃神谕，找到另一条「再创世」的道路……

**Khaslana:** Because I know both of you, and I believe that no matter how the course of history may change...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**因为我相信：无论历史的进程如何改变，我认识的你们——

**Khaslana:** The faith we all share will always remain the same.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我们所有人的信念，必定始终如一。

**Khaslana:** And so, the layers of rock began to reverse, and history wove itself anew.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**于是，岩层开始倒转，历史重新罗织。

**Khaslana:** Through my old acquaintances, I was given the chance to meet the leader of the Flame-Chase Journey, Cerydra. And after a long and arduous negotiation, we came to an agreement—

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在故人引荐下，我得见逐火之旅的领导者，刻律德菈。一场艰难而漫长的谈判后，我们达成共识——

**Khaslana:** The Imperator's alliance needed the Destruction, and I was ready to pour all my strength into it. The spoils of the Flame-Chase — the Coreflames — would be sealed by my hand.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**凯撒的同盟需要「毁灭」，我愿意倾尽全力。而逐火的战利品——火种——都将为我封印。

**Khaslana:** But the condition was this: In the upcoming battle, in our conquest of the "Earth"...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**前提是：在即将到来的下一场战役，对「大地」的征伐中……

**Khaslana:** I had to prove that my power could transcend fate.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我能证明，自己的力量足以超越命运。

**Khaslana:** In the original history, it was a brutal war. At the end, the Titankin of Georios, Terravox, killed the rampaging Titan and took the Coreflame that supported the earth.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在原本的历史中，那是一场惨烈的战争。最终，吉奥里亚的眷属「荒笛」弑杀发狂的泰坦，接过支撑大地的火种。

**Khaslana:** But this time, Amphoreus encountered a turning point in its fate.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**但这一次，翁法罗斯迎来了命运的转折。

**Khaslana:** Neither side suffered any losses. When I severed the Titan's head and raised the Coreflame high, the crowd gathered around the scorched remains of the god, roaring with joy...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**双方未有一人牺牲。当我斩下巨神头颅，将火种高高举起，众人围绕那具被金血焚灭的神骸，欢声雷动——

**Khaslana:** Cheering for the hero of the Destruction, who had ignited the faint spark of life for the first time.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**——欢呼「毁灭」的英雄，初次点燃生命的微光。

**Khaslana:** Yes, in that flickering light, the once-dark path ahead seemed to become clearer.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**是啊，在摇曳的微光中，原本晦暗的前路仿佛也变得有迹可循。

**Khaslana:** I proved myself, and in the long years that followed, I brought down the other Titans, one by one.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我证明了自己，也在随后的漫长时间里，为其余泰坦一一带去陨落。

**Khaslana:** I could do more than just wait. When time resets, the chance to change the world also emerges from the lines of history...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我能做的不止有「等待」，当时间回到原点，改变世界的契机，也会从历史的字里行间浮现……

**Khaslana:** Until, finally, the brutal future revealed by "Era Nova" is reversed.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**直到，逆转「再创世」揭示的残酷未来。

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我以「愤怒」铭记此世的全部。只要我还在燃烧，他们就从未离去。

> **白厄：**——拔剑吧，刽子手！我会让你知道，你最大的错误，就是留下了我！

...

**Khaslana:** After that, he raised his sword and attacked...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在那之后，他举剑袭来……

> **卡厄斯兰那：**然后，卡厄斯兰那杀死了自己。

> **卡厄斯兰那：**那一天，是谁倒在剑下，留在过去；又是谁前往未来，我已经记不清了。

**Khaslana:** I only remember a nymph, hiding in the shadows just out of sight, futilely pushing a stone ball...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我只记得一只若虫，它藏在余光尚不能及的阴影中，徒劳地推着石球……

**Khaslana:** Climb, fall, climb, fall, climb again, and fall...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**攀上，落下；攀上，落下；攀上，落下……

**Khaslana:** The only freedom the nymph had was in choosing how to roll the stone: sometimes fast, sometimes slow, sometimes resting on a gentler slope, leaning against the stone for a break...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**若虫拥有的自由，只在于决定以何种方式推动圆石：时快，时慢，时而停留在一处不那么陡峭的斜坡上，倚靠圆石小憩……

**Khaslana:** But its choice couldn't change the futility of it all: the stone would always roll back down the slope, and the nymph would always return to the starting point, beginning again.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**但它的选择无法改变「徒劳」的本质：圆石总会从斜坡上滚下，若虫也总会回到斜坡的起点，重新开始。

**Khaslana:** After that, every time I reached this point in the cycle, the burning sky would cast a shadow over its path. It would nearly reach the peak at the fourth quint of Curtain-Fall Hour, then fall to the starting point in the first fifteen seconds of the next Entry Hour.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**那之后，每一个轮回，我行至此地时，燃烧的天空总会为它的行迹投下影子。它会在幕匿时的第四个时刻抵达顶点附近，下一个门扉时的第一个十五秒摔落起点。

**Khaslana:** And my efforts in this cycle would dissolve into nothing at the same time.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**而后，我在这次轮回中的一切努力，也会在同一时间化为泡影。

### Talk to Anaxa

> *Do you still remember them, Khaslana, the one who won't reach the dawn? Once, your teacher could still guide you through the dark. And that fleet-footed traveler, the thief of Dolos, her home was yet untouched by the fires you brought. Yes, the past is gone. But you, godlike warrior, did you ever shed a tear for those years, sweet as wine? Weren't you once bound by blood and flesh? Wasn't golden blood thicker than water? No! Let your tears flow freely. Don't let rage burn them away — Then, remember to drink deep: For, to the gods, the tears of mortals are sweeter than any honey brew on Earth.*

**Khaslana:** Of course. When I first set out on the Flame-Chase Journey, Lady Tribbie never managed to teach me Oronyx's Miracle...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**当然。最初踏上逐火之旅时，缇宝老师怎么也教不会我的欧洛尼斯神迹……

**Khaslana:** ...But along the way, I learned a thing or two.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…一路走来，我也总算略知一二了。

### Perform Oronyx's Miracle to take back the Coreflame

*(Perform Oronyx's Miracle)*

**Cipher:** Where... Where are you going?

> **赛飞儿：**你、你去哪儿？

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** I... I don't know?

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**我、我不知道啊…？

*(After Bartholos is brought back)*

**Anaxa:** Heh, the Venerationists' foolish inventions finally come in handy.

> **那刻夏：**哼，敬拜学派的愚蠢发明也算是派上用场了。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Huff... Huff... How is this possible?

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**呼哧…呼哧…怎么会这样……

**Khaslana:** You've arrived, Zagreus... Always so punctual.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**你来了，扎格列斯…还是如此准时。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Wh—What? Do you know who I am?

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**什、什么，你知道本大爷…？

**Khaslana:** I'm running out of patience, so I'm not going to waste words. Listen up, Spirithief: Hand over the Coreflame, and let's pretend we never clashed. This way, you and this world can still be saved.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我的耐心已经快用尽了，所以更不想浪费口舌。听好，贼灵：交出火种，就当我们从未交锋过。这样，你们和这个世界都能得到拯救。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Heh, heh, heh, "save"? You sing better than any bard!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**桀桀桀，说得比吟游诗人唱得还好听！说什么「拯救」……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** If you ask me... you're the greatest disaster Amphoreus has ever known!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**要本大爷说…你自己就是翁法罗斯天大的祸患！

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** No matter how strong you are, you're still just a mortal body! Carrying twelve Coreflames? Don't make me laugh! You'll just get consumed by the divine flames, and drag this world down with you...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**就算你再怎么厉害，也不过是肉体凡胎罢了！承载十二颗火种？别开玩笑了，你只会被神火吞噬，然后拉这个世界给你当陪葬……

**Khaslana:** ...One hundred thirty-four.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…一百三十四。

**Anaxa:** What...

> **那刻夏：**什么……

**Khaslana:** This is the 134th cycle I've lived through. And the number of Coreflames I carry is...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**这是我经历的第一百三十四次轮回。已为我所承载的火种数量，是……

**Anaxa:** ...1,596.

> **那刻夏：**…一千五百九十六。

**Khaslana:** ...Yes.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…嗯。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** A thousand Coreflames, and just a single Chrysos Heir... How is that even possible!?

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**一千多颗火种，区区一个黄金裔…怎么可能？！

**Khaslana:** I've come this far, and that's proof enough.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我能一路走到这里，就是证明。

**Anaxa:** But someday... you will burn to the ground.

> **那刻夏：**可总有一天…你会被焚烧殆尽。

**Khaslana:** Of course, the divine flames will eventually consume my eyes and blind me...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**当然，神火总有一天会烧毁我的眼球，令我盲目……

**Khaslana:** But before that, they could still forge and sharpen Dawnmaker. Now, when I cut open your stomach, you won't feel a thing.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**但在那之前，它们还能为「侵晨」淬火。这样在我剖开你的胃囊时，你就不会感受到痛苦。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** You... you...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**你、你……

**Khaslana:** This is the final warning.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**这是最后通牒。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Heh, heh... Fine, fine...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**桀桀桀…好啊，好啊……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Assume this world holds a hundred million possibilities, and no matter how one looks at it, ninety-nine million, nine hundred ninety-nine thousand, nine hundred and ninety-nine of them all point to outcomes of certain Destruction...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**假设这个世界的未来存在一亿种可能性，不管怎么看，那其中九千九百九十九万九千九百九十九种都指向了「毁灭」的结局……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Even so, I'll use my divine power, despised by the people of this world, to achieve that one-in-a-hundred-million possibility...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**就算这样，本大爷也会用遭世人唾弃的神力，实现那亿分之一的可能……

**Cipher:** ...Bartholos!

> **赛飞儿：**…巴特鲁斯！

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Brat... if you've got the guts, you'll have to first step over my dead body!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**小鬼…你要是真有种，就先跨过本大爷的尸体…！

### Defeat Spirithief Bartholos to take back the Coreflame

*(Begin battle against "Spirithief" Bartholos ×20)*

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Heh, heh, heh! Cat-eared thief, run! Go find Aglaea...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**桀桀桀！猫耳小贼，快逃，去找阿格莱雅……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** This little brat... is not a threat we can deal with just by ourselves!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**这小鬼…绝不是你我二人联手能解决的威胁…！

### Confront Cipher

**Anaxa:** Heh, how absurd...

> **那刻夏：**呵，真是荒唐……

**Khaslana:** In my memories, Zagreus always meets the same end, over and over again...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在我的记忆里，扎格列斯总是在重复自己的结局……

**Khaslana:** Their departure no longer stirs anything in my heart.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**它的离席，已经不会在我心中掀起波澜了。

**Anaxa:** This "Reason" Coreflame before us is a fake, obvious as day.

> **那刻夏：**眼前这枚「理性」的火种是赝品，一目了然。

**Khaslana:** Ah... I know.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**啊…我知道。

**Khaslana:** I know. It always plays out like this. The Spirithief covers for her, and the "Trickery" demigod escapes with two Coreflames...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我知道，总会这样。在贼灵的掩护下，「诡计」半神会带着两枚火种逃之夭夭……

**Cipher:** ...

> **赛飞儿：**……

**Khaslana:** ...And then, driven by defiance, or hatred, she decides to stand before me once more.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…然后，出于不甘，或是仇恨，再度站在我的面前。

**Khaslana:** Exactly like the countless past hers I've known... cunning, yet so naive.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**就像我过去认识的无数个她们…既狡猾，又天真。

**Cipher:** Since you've witnessed this same scene play out countless times, tell me...

> **赛飞儿：**既然同样的情节已经在你眼中上演了无数次，告诉我……

**Cipher:** Will you carve the "Trickery" Coreflame from my chest now?

> **赛飞儿：**接下来…你会把「诡计」的火种从我胸口剜出来吗？

**Khaslana:** ...That depends on your attitude. At least so far, in the cycles where you've asked this question... the endings haven't been so cruel.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**…这取决于你的态度。至少目前为止，在你问出了这句话的那些轮回里…结局没有那么残忍。

**Cipher:** Heh... well, thank you for that. But I also believe that this time, you won't catch up to me—

> **赛飞儿：**哼…那还真是谢谢你啊。但我同样相信，这一次你追不上我——

**Cipher:** No one can catch up to me. I'm no longer running to escape anymore.

> **赛飞儿：**没人能追得上我，我早就不再为「逃亡」而奔跑了。

**Khaslana:** Whether you believe me or not, I'll still tell you this: You've made the wrong decision.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**不论你相信与否，我都会告诉你，这是个错误的决定。

**Cipher:** Alright, then, we'll see—

> **赛飞儿：**好啊，那就试试看——

**Anaxa:** Silence.

> **那刻夏：**——肃静。

**Khaslana:** Ah... this is new.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**啊…这倒是头一遭。

**Cipher:** ...One-eyed boy, what are you trying to pull now?

> **赛飞儿：**…独眼男孩，你这又是唱的哪一出？

**Anaxa:** Go, Cifera. Give us some time in private.

> **那刻夏：**你走吧，赛法利娅。给我们一点私人时间。

**Anaxa:** This isn't for her, but for you.

> **那刻夏：**这不是为她，而是为你。

**Anaxa:** You've walked too far down this path. It's time to discuss a possibility: Maybe the road you're walking is not one of salvation, but one that will simply drag the world into the flames.

> **那刻夏：**你已经在这条路上走得太远，该讨论一种可能性了：也许你所行的根本不是拯救的道路，只是单纯地把这世界拖入火海而已。

**Anaxa:** For old times' sake, turn back. I don't care how many times you've tried, because there's one thing I'm sure of—

> **那刻夏：**看在你我也曾为师生的份上，回头吧。我不在乎你已经尝试了多少次，因为有一件事我已十分明了——

**Anaxa:** Your messiah complex has turned you into something no different from the icy gods you spoke of. The children of humanity you swore to save... they are no more than ants to you now, aren't they?

> **那刻夏：**你心底的救世主情结，已将你变得与你口中冷眼的神明并无区别。那些你誓言要拯救的人子…如今在你眼里，他们的性命恐怕与蝼蚁无异吧？

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

**Cipher:** Heh, of course.

> **赛飞儿：**呵，怪不得。

**Cipher:** I've been wondering, what kind of demon could burn my homeland to the ground in an instant, without batting an eye...

> **赛飞儿：**我每天都在想，究竟是什么样的恶魔才能在顷刻间烧毁我的家乡，连眼都不眨一下……

**Cipher:** Now, I have the answer. It turns out that fiery demon has been fantasizing of becoming this world's Deliverer!

> **赛飞儿：**现在，我找到答案了。原来那浑身着火的恶魔，满脑子幻想的都是要成为「救世主」哪！

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

**Khaslana:** I don't want to argue anymore. No matter what I say, the outcome won't change.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我已经不想再辩解什么了。不管付出多少口舌，结局都不会改变。

**Khaslana:** Make your biggest effort to resist, or accept fate and hand over the Coreflame to me...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**做出你们最大的努力抗争，或是接受命运，将火种交给我……

**Khaslana:** Even if it's futile, you... we all have the right to choose.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**哪怕只是徒劳，你们…我们，都有选择的权利。

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** Yes, even if it is futile, and the world is just a divine joke...

> **那刻夏：**没错，纵使这只是「徒劳」，世界不过是一场神明的玩笑……

**Cipher:** When I walk into the "Destruction" written by the gods with my own two legs, you will witness—

> **赛飞儿：**但当我靠着自己的双腿，踏入被诸神写就的「毁灭」中时，你会见证——

**Cipher:** Even the smallest footnote still leaves a mark in the story!

> **赛飞儿：**哪怕是微不足道的注脚，也会在故事里留下自己的印记！

**Khaslana:** And so, at the end of the 134th cycle...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**于是，在第一百三十四次轮回的尽头……

**Khaslana:** The "Traveler" and the "Scholar" fulfilled their fates once again.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**「羁客」与「学士」兑现了他们的命运，再一次。

**Khaslana:** Or perhaps, it wasn't just they who fulfilled their fates. I can't quite remember all this.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**或者，兑现命运的不止他们。只是我已记不清了。

**Khaslana:** In the endless darkness, for no reason, a voice always lingered by my ear...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**无边的黑暗中，没有来由地，一道声音始终在耳边萦绕……

**Khaslana:** "Losses are a constant on the Flame-Chase journey, among which even life itself holds little value."

> **卡厄斯兰那：**「逐火是不断失却的旅途，在那一切当中，生命也微不足惜。」

**Khaslana:** ...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**……

**Khaslana:** "Reduced to ashes"... Such a familiar end.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**「付之一炬」…多么熟悉的结局。

**Khaslana:** It happens in every journey, always the same.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**它也出现在每一段旅途里，始终如一。

### "You will bear the blazing sun, until the pale dawn breaks."

> *Do you still remember him, Khaslana, the one who won't reach the dawn? All hail Mydeimos, the greatest conqueror, the mightiest protector, the strongest of all. The dying lion, eyes blazing, the king with no kingdom. Tell me, if Kephale never forgets — how many scars have you already carved on him? How many vertebrae have you shattered? How many times did you drive your blade through his back? How did the Library of Garbaniphoro fall to flame? What color was the destruction of the last Kremnoan? You pitiful jester, fit not only for singing, dancing, and jests, but also for treachery that walks the razor's edge. Go on, veil your folly with the weight of the world, then adorn your fratricide with it — just to savor the taste of tragedy.*

>>> Eternal Recurrence #108642:

*(Cutscene begins)*

33,441,694 Eternal Recurrences until the end of the world

*(Cutscene ends)*

The 108,642nd Eternal Recurrence

**Mydei:** ...You don't look so good, Deliverer.

> **万敌：**…这姿态真是丑陋啊，「救世主」。

**Khaslana:** Even after going through 108,642 cycles...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**即便度过了十万八千六百四十二个轮回……

**Khaslana:** Mydei... your name for me has never changed.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**万敌…你对我的称呼始终没有变过。

**Mydei:** Why do you sound so pleased? It's a derogatory name.

> **万敌：**得意什么？这是蔑称。

**Khaslana:** Yeah... I know all too well: you only meant it sincerely in the first cycle.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**是啊…我再清楚不过了：不是蔑称的，只有第一次轮回而已。

**Khaslana:** After that one and only time we walked side by side, every time since, it always ends up like this...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在那唯一一次并肩同行后，每一次，我们总会像这样……

**Khaslana:** Dawnmaker will stab you through your tenth thoracic vertebra. That's your weak spot and the only way to kill you.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**「侵晨」会刺入你的第十节胸椎，那是唯一能杀死你的弱点。

**Mydei:** Interesting. Tell me, when the Mydeimoses of the past faced you, did they ever retreat?

> **万敌：**有趣。告诉我，往日的迈德漠斯在面对你时，可曾退缩过？

**Khaslana:** Never.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**从未有过。

**Mydei:** Then they died honorably, worthy of the name of "Strife."

> **万敌：**那他们死得其所，无愧「纷争」之名。

**Mydei:** But there's still tenderness in your eyes. How pathetic.

> **万敌：**但你的眼中仍有恻隐，实在可悲。

**Khaslana:** I... have to remember how this feels.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我…必须记住这份感受。

**Khaslana:** Remember that I was once human, or else the Destruction will completely consume me.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**记住我本生而为人，以免「毁灭」将我彻底吞噬。

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Mydei:** If you win, tell the Mydeimos of the next life: This person is a worthy opponent, so always remember to give it your all and never slack off.

> **万敌：**如果你能胜出，去告诉来世的迈德漠斯：此人是可敬的对手，切记使出全力，不得怠慢。

**Khaslana:** I'll keep that in mind... as always.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我会铭记在心…一如既往。

**Mydei:** Even though the dynasty is left with just one, the Kremnos Festival will continue as usual. I, the king of the Kremnoan legion, will host the final feast in the center of this arena, and the prize will be the flames of "Strife."

> **万敌：**即使王朝仅余一人，悬锋祭典也将如常。朕，悬锋众军之王，就在这竞技场中心摆设最后一场盛宴，奖赏便是「纷争」的烈火。

**Mydei:** "One day, you shall die with a wound in your back." That's what I saw when I overcame the trial, the gods' dying ramblings...

> **万敌：**「终有一日，汝将背后负创而死」，这是我战胜试炼时望见的，诸神临终的谵语……

**Mydei:** Enough, you've probably heard this thousands of times. So you must understand, facing the demigod of "Strife" means...

> **万敌：**罢了，想必这话你已听过无数次。所以你一定也明白，面对「纷争」的半神……

**Mydei:** There's no way you'll take this Coreflame in peace.

> **万敌：**你绝无可能以和平的姿态取走这枚火种。

**Khaslana:** I understand...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**我明白……

**Mydei:** Step forward and face me in a final battle!

> **万敌：**踏上前来，与我决一死战吧！

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** I, the last son of Gorgo, the demigod of "Strife," salute you—

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**我，最后的歌耳戈之子，「纷争」之半神，向你致敬——

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** I'll give ten thousand scars and ten thousand lives of mine to fuel the blazing sun of deliverance!

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**以我的千万道伤疤和性命——为救世的烈阳添光！

*(Begin battle against The Giver, Master of Legions, Lance of Fury ×20)*

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** O martyrs, behold! The last Kremnos Festival has begun—

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**众英灵啊，看吧！悬锋最后的祭典已经开场——

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** And will not end till death!

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**——至死方休！

*(When Mydeimos, Lance of Fury uses The Vanquished Behold Woe)*

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** Conquer this world, or be conquered by me—

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**征服此世，或受我征服——

**Khaslana:** As you wish...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**如你所愿……

**Khaslana:** For the sake of fairness, let us go to a vaster battleground—

> **卡厄斯兰那：**为了公平，让我们奔赴更辽阔的疆场——

*(When Phainon uses He Who Bears the World Must Burn)*

**Khaslana:** And in my full glory, I salute you!

> **卡厄斯兰那：**并以全盛之姿，向你致敬！——

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** That's all I ever wanted!

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**——求之不得！

### "You will bear the blazing sun, until... dawn breaks..."

> *Do you still remember her, Khaslana, the one who won't reach the dawn? Castorice, with every sigh flowing from her fingertips and her hands blocking all words of comfort, she held each death close to her heart and swallowed every cry of grief in her palms. Tell me, if Kephale never forgets, how many pairs of wings have you shattered for her? How many blossoms have you snuffed out? How many butterflies have you cast into the ashes? The moonlight's fragrance once streamed like the River of Souls. How did you turn it into golden poison and make it libations for your banquet of futile deliverance? And yet it is you, so-called Deliverer in sheep's clothing, who ought to be the sacrifice upon the altar to the gods! Ugly beast — your seemingly pious gaze cannot hide the seething venom beneath. And that spear-like horn of yours was born to slay your kin. So feast upon bones and call it grace. The sweetness will surely convince you: "I never truly knew anything about the blade of slaughter."*

*(Cutscene begins)*

### Bid farewell to The Hero Within

> *Do you still remember, Khaslana, the one who won't reach the dawn? In that drunken dream, the mountains loomed high and steep — and there, you saw yourself. Believe me, O future witness — he stood atop that peak, and the people sang with him in ecstasy. The broken Chrysos Heirs, the twisted demigods — they sang, they spun, they stamped their feet upon the ground with him. Hail! Terror struck his heart: Prophecy burned in every soul, wild with rapture and madness. Hail! Spare them, Khaslana, spare them — your peerless flame sends chills down every spine! Let them sing of the tireless heroes, sing of the Era Nova, sing of that land where wine and honey flow in torrents. Let them sing how he wove new glory among the stars, and how the Eternal City crashed to dust. How Okhemans, Castrum Kremnos, and every lineage they knew were destroyed. As for Khaslana — He is still within the drunken dreams, upon the sheer and jagged peak! There he lights flame with blood, and blood with flame, like a sun whose blaze banished all evil from its path. The mountains rise cruel and steep. Yet still he dances there alone, wild and unceasing in his joy. Yet all proves futile — the flames know no weariness, and dawn is beyond reach. All proves futile — the flames know no weariness, and dawn is beyond reach.*

*(Cutscene begins)*
